Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the satellites framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 873. The resulting operator achieves bound O(448/n^5).

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_873(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 873. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^448(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 220.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For any satellites module M satisfying the structural decomposition hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 926. The projective resolution has length at most 852 with Betti numbers bounded by 4.
